## Aging

'I found a bear by the stream,' said the minister, 'and preached God's holy word. The bear was so mesmerized that he let me baptize him.' They both look down at the rabbi, who was lying on a gurney in a body cast. 'Maybe I shouldn't have started with the circumcision.'

• Researchers for the Massachusetts Turnpike recently found 200 dead crows and there was an immediate concern about Avian Flu. A pathologist examined the crows and found the cause of death to be vehicular impacts. They then determined that 98% of the vehicles were trucks, and only 2% were cars. They then hired an Ornithological Behaviorist to determine the cause of that disproportionate percentage, and he quickly determined the cause. When crows eat road kill, they always have a look-out crow in a nearby tree to warn of danger. They discovered that while all the lookout crows could shout 'Cah!', not a single one could shout 'Truck!'

## Religion

• A priest, a minister, and a rabbi want to see who's best at his job, so they each go into the woods, find a bear, and attempt to convert it and report back. The priest said, 'When I found a bear, I read to him from the Catechism and sprinkled him with holy water. Next week is his first communion.' 'I found a bear by the stream,' said the minister, 'and preached God's holy word. The bear was so mesmerized that he let me baptize him.' They both look down at the rabbi, who was lying on a gurney in a body cast. 'Maybe I shouldn't have started with the circumcision.'

'That’s my house,' says the castaway. 'What’s the second hut for?' 'That’s my church.' 'And the third hut?' 'Oh, that?' sniffs the castaway. 'That’s the church I used to go to.'

## Zombies

• It's good to keep an open mind - just don't let your brains fall out.

• What is the zombie's favorite type of bean? A human bean

• Why don't zombies eat clowns? They taste funny.
• Zombies eat brains. You are safe.  (Jon Stewart)
